the code and tests in this tree. Original sweep: debt-sweep worktree, HEAD
`14bc2cb6`, dated 2026-09-12. **Gap-closure pass** (historical sweep): same
worktree, starting HEAD `c287f5fb`, also dated 2026-09-12 — an independent
verifier found coverage gaps in the first pass (missing items across all
five design-doc ledgers, stale line-number citations, and one row,

completion pass** (historical sweep): same worktree, starting HEAD
`56992663`, also dated 2026-09-12 — a second independent verifier found
the tree otherwise accurate and two remaining gaps: one IMPROVEMENTS.md
heading (the GGX low-F0 grazing gain) not yet carried into the table as

and tests the way `## Already resolved` requires (now DL-R18..DL-R23, one
of which — DL-R20 — turned out to be resolved-in-code but only indirectly
tested, noted as such rather than silently closed); see `## Counts` for
the delta. **Re-sweep** (this pass): debt-resweep worktree, HEAD
`876c9a26`, dated 2026-09-12 — run after a separate cleanup session closed
16 rows (DL-01/02/03/04/34/36/37/48/50/51/54/55/56/57/58/59) and added
DL-38..DL-62 without a further independent check. This pass re-verified
all 47 rows left OPEN against `876c9a26`'s actual source, cross-checked
against the cleanup session's touched-file list, and found the ledger's
OPEN/CLOSED partition already correct (0 rows reclassified); one stale
citation on DL-19 was corrected in place (see `## Counts`). Every verdict

Sorted by class (physics-bias/energy-loss, precision, API/bridge gap,
coverage/test gap, perf, doc-rot), S before M before L within a class (the

| DL-67 | Found during DL-42/DL-43 (debt-guiding slice, 2026-09-13; filed in this slice's own ledger as DL-65, RENUMBERED DL-67 at merge time -- see `## Counts` -- to avoid colliding with rows concurrently filed by sibling debt-cleanup slices: DL-65 is the ggx slice's row, DL-66 the sssenv slice's, DL-68 the translucent slice's) | PT's and BDPT's guided one-sample and RIS proposal/throughput densities are inconsistent across lobe-selection branches at a multi-lobe SPF: neither RIS candidate 0 nor the one-sample/RIS "aggregate BSDF" branches use the TRUE generating density of the direction they price | OPEN-confirmed (static + closed-form counterexample; rendered red-proof pending) | Root cause: at a multi-lobe SPF, candidate 0's (and any kray-based branch's) TRUE generating density for a direction is the mixture `sum_I q_I * p_I` over every lobe `Scatter()` could have emitted, where `q_I = PTScatterSelectWeight(lobe_I) / sum_j PTScatterSelectWeight(lobe_j)` (`PathTracingIntegrator.cpp:1337`, `MaxValue(kray)`-weighted) -- NOT the selected lobe's own `pS->pdf`, and NOT the material's aggregate `ISPF::Pdf()` either, because `ISPF::Pdf()` may re-weight lobes by a different ratio than `PTRandomlySelect` used to pick one. (The original example here was `SchlickSPF::Pdf`, which weighted diffuse/specular by `MaxValue(rd)` vs `MaxValue(rs)` -- the raw painter albedos -- while `PTScatterSelectWeight`/`PTRandomlySelect` weight the SAME two lobes by `MaxValue(diffuse kray)` vs `MaxValue(rho+(1-rho)*fresnel)`. **That example is HISTORICAL as of 2026-09-14**: Slice 0 replaced it with the true generating density (see the Slice 0 note at the end of this cell), so `SchlickSPF::Pdf` is no longer an instance. `IsotropicPhongSPF::Pdf` -- symbol, not line number -- still is: DL-98.). So: (1) candidate 0 (`c.bsdfPdf = pS->pdf`, `PathTracingIntegrator.cpp:3137`; `BDPTIntegrator.cpp:2537` eye / `:6276` light) uses the wrong (single-lobe) density, and candidate 1's aggregate `PTEvalPdfAtSurface`/`PathValueOps::EvalPdfAtVertex` uses a DIFFERENT wrong (aggregate-but-mismatched-weights) density -- both miss the true `sum_I q_I p_I`, so RIS's `sum_i P_i/D_i = M` identity is violated: a closed-form 2-candidate example with `p1=10, p2=0.1` (per-lobe pdfs), guide pdf `p_g=1`, true aggregate `p_agg=5.05` gives per-branch coefficients 1.074 (candidate-0-style) vs 0.256 (aggregate-style) against the correct value, i.e. roughly a 33% low estimate using the true generating density as ground truth. (2) The same inconsistency exists between PT's THREE one-sample/RIS overwrite branches in `PathTracingIntegrator.cpp`'s trained-guiding block: branch (a), this slice's DL-42 fix (`PTScatterKray(*pS) * pS->pdf/(selectProb*combinedPdf)`, per-lobe kray/pdf); branch (c), the one-sample guided-direction-accepted branch (`PTMulDiv(fGuided,cosTheta,combinedPdf)`, aggregate f over aggregate-but-mismatched pdf); and the RIS-accepted branch (b). (a) and (c) do not partition to a consistent total unless every lobe shares one pdf (single-lobe SPF) -- adding `/q_I` to (c) does not fix this; the correct construction uses one `f` and one denominator (the true mixture density `sum_I q_I p_I` over the lobes `Scatter()` actually accepted) in ALL THREE branches, in both PT and BDPT eye/light. Scope: any multi-emit SPF (SchlickSPF, PolishedSPF/PolishedBRDF, TranslucentSPF, CompositeSPF, BioSpecSkinSPF, GenericHumanTissueSPF); `GGXSPF`/`CoatedSPF` are immune (internal single-lobe selection: exactly one `ScatteredRay` per `Scatter()` call, `pdf==mixPdf`, outer `selectProb` trivially 1). A separate, additive asymmetry in the same candidate machinery: candidate 1 is discarded (`c.valid=false`, `risWeight=0`) whenever the material's aggregate pdf is 0 at the guided direction (`PathTracingIntegrator.cpp:3148` candidate 0 / `:3177` candidate 1 / `:3191` fallback; `BDPTIntegrator.cpp:2547`/`:2584`/`:2598` eye, `:6286`/`:6314`/`:6328` light), silently falling back to candidate 0 alone and losing whatever variance-reduction the guide would have offered there -- energy-losing in the sense of discarding a legitimate RIS candidate, tracked as part of the same rework rather than a fourth bug. Recipe: red-prove on `schlick_material` (diffuse+specular, both eligible, overlapping support) with guiding forced to substitute (`guidingAlpha` near 1, a real trained field so `guidedSubstituted>0`, unlike this slice's DL-42 fixture which forced `guidedSubstituted=0`), comparing rendered radiance against unguided PT reference; fix = the one-f/one-denominator construction above in PT (all three branches) and BDPT eye/light RIS. **Slice 0 landed 2026-09-14** (`docs/DL67_SLICE0_SCHLICK_PDF_WEIGHTS.md`, branch `debt-slice0`): `SchlickSPF::Pdf`/`PdfNM` now returns the ACTUAL density of the direction `Scatter` + `RandomlySelect` produce -- the `ISPF::Pdf()` half of this row's own root-cause paragraph. The diffuse lobe's coefficient `C_D = E_{(u,v)}[P(the diffuse ray wins | that specular draw)]` is computed by a DETERMINISTIC 16x16 stratified quadrature that REPLAYS `GenerateSpecularRay` in its own `(xi,b)` inverse-CDF parametrisation (sharing `SchlickSampleHalfVector` with the sampler, and reproducing Scatter's accept-checks and `RandomlySelect`'s `freeidx==1`/underflow branches); the specular coefficient is exact at the query direction; the per-channel branch's three lanes (which share ONE random pair) are handled exactly FOR THE INTENDED SEMANTICS by inverting the sampler at the query direction and replaying the other two lanes (the sampler's own per-channel branch deviates from that intended semantics while **DL-101** is open, per the round-2 review -- see the doc's SS2 and SS7); and a tilted shading normal is modelled by the exact clipped-cosine-hemisphere fraction `A_D=(1+dot(n,geomN))/2`. **This CORRECTS, not extends, the first version of this slice**, which used `MaxValue(rd)/(MaxValue(rd)+SchlickFresnelAvg(rs))` for `C_D`: that is the wrong quantity three times over (measured, see the doc's §3 table): the hemispherical average is under the wrong measure (mild -- 0.9009 vs 0.9048 at `rs=0.9`, but 0.1961 vs 0.0667 at `rs=0.02`/80 degrees); `C_D = E[w_D/(w_D+w_S)]` is a CONVEX function of `w_S`, so substituting any average of `w_S` is a different number by Jensen regardless; and the term that actually dominates is not a reflectance at all but the specular sampler's REJECTION rate -- `Scatter` accepts only 0.739/0.640/0.577 of its specular draws at 30/60/80 degrees incidence, and a rejected specular draw leaves `RandomlySelect` holding one ray, returned with probability 1. Even a rejection-aware closed form (`A*rd/(rd+rs)+(1-A)`) still misses true `C_D` by up to 13.7%, so there was no closed form to reach for. Measured, the first version's aggregate integrated to 0.68-1.02 instead of 1 and its total variation against the real sampler was WORSE than the code it replaced on 6 of the 8 review configurations (by up to 2.4x), a wash on a seventh and better on the eighth. Its two gates could not see this: one compared `Pdf()` against a replica of its own formula, the other asserted `Pdf(w_S) >= q_S p_S`, an identity for any non-negative `C_D`. Both were deleted. Red-proof is now a two-sided normalisation gate (a hemisphere quadrature of `Pdf` against the MEASURED probability that `Scatter` emits any ray, over 600k real calls) plus a total-variation gate against a 600k-draw histogram of real `Scatter`+`RandomlySelect` draws, over 12 configurations, RGB and NM: `Checks: 34 Failures: 34` at `4325f365`, `33` at pre-slice `32824325`, **`0` after**, with every `|int Pdf - emitted|` <= 0.0062 and every TVD <= 0.0081 against a derived MC-noise floor of 0.0051. Two further defects of the same pattern were found while measuring and fixed in the same commit: the anisotropic AZIMUTHAL DENSITY was a different distribution from the one the sampler draws (up to 25x off at isotropy 0.3, measured against a 2e6-draw histogram; recorded as **DL-102**, closed), and `ComputeSchlickSpecularPdf` built its half-vector in a FlipW'd frame on the false premise that `GenerateSpecularRay` does (that function's `onb` parameter was dead; it always used `ri.onb`). `SPFPdfConsistencyTest`'s Schlick row consequently tightened from a 15% integral tolerance to the standard 5% (measured 1.00166/0.999932) and its chi2 is now GATED (783.7/816.3 vs critical 928.3) instead of skipped; its old note blaming "the specular PDF normalisation assumes isotropic roughness" was a misdiagnosis and is corrected in place. Cost: `Pdf()` 17.0 ns -> 690 ns (2003 ns on the 3-lane per-channel branch); whole-render CPU +2.5% (inside a +/-4% spread) on a Cornell box where 1 of 15 objects is schlick, +26% on a variant where every surface is. Rendered frame-mean luminance on that scene moved 0.995781 +/- 0.000318 -> 0.997451 +/- 0.000065 over 4 runs each (+0.168%). This does NOT close the row: candidate 0/1's own PT/BDPT branch-level fix (this row's actual subject) is unstarted. Sibling audit opened DL-98, DL-99, DL-100, DL-101 and closed DL-102; `CookTorranceSPF`/`GGXSPF`/`CoatedSPF`/`PolishedSPF`/Ward/`SchlickBRDF` confirmed immune or already correct on THIS row's pattern. **2026-09-14 (debt-dl69 slice, DL-69 closed):** the shared root cause's ORDINARY (non-guiding) half is now fixed -- BDPT/VCM/MLT's unconditional non-delta throughput pairs the selected lobe's own `kray_I` with its own `selectProb`, and `pdfFwd` is the aggregate `ISPF::Pdf()` that `pdfRev` already used. That slice deliberately did NOT touch either guided branch: when guiding SUBSTITUTED a direction, `BDPTIntegrator.cpp`'s eye and light generators still price the aggregate `EvalBSDFAtVertex` over `selectProb * guidedEffectivePdf`. **`pdfFwd` there is NOT "still `selectProb * effectivePdf`", as this cell claimed until the 2026-09-14 review**: the new `pdfFwdPrev` block carries no `usedGuidedDirection` guard, so under substitution `pdfFwd` is the material's AGGREGATE `ISPF::Pdf()` evaluated at the GUIDED direction (`scatDir`, which the substitution overwrote with `guidedDir`), with `selectProb * effectivePdf` surviving only as the `misFwdPdf <= NEARZERO` fallback. That is a THIRD density -- neither the guided sampling density nor a mixture that includes the guide -- so DL-67's own reconciliation has to account for it rather than assume the branch was left alone -- so DL-67's scope is unchanged except that its §2.5 "define candidate 0's `bsdfPdf` via `PTEvalPdfAtSurface`" step now has a same-file precedent to match (the `pdfFwdPrev` block in both generators). What DID change under DL-67's feet: the guiding-BLENDED-density branch (`bsdfCombinedPdf > NEARZERO`, the kept-BSDF-direction case) now takes PT's own DL-42-fixed per-lobe shape `kray_I * pScat->pdf / (selectProb * combinedPdf)` instead of the aggregate form, so BDPT and PT agree there and DL-67 no longer needs to reconcile them. | M-L | physics-bias | user-visible (guided renders of any multi-lobe, multi-emit material) |
| ~~DL-69~~ | ~~Found during DL-42/DL-67 derivation (debt-guiding slice, 2026-09-13)~~ | ~~BDPT's ORDINARY (non-guiding, unconditional) eye/light non-delta throughput pairs the material's AGGREGATE (all-lobes-summed) BSDF value with a PER-LOBE selection pdf, over-counting by up to Nx at a multi-lobe SPF whose lobes have overlapping support~~ Both generators now pair the SELECTED lobe's own `kray_I` with that lobe's own selection probability -- the delta branch's and PT's shipped formula -- and store the material's AGGREGATE, direction-only `ISPF::Pdf()` as `pdfFwd`, the same function `pdfRev` and every connection strategy already evaluate | CLOSED 2026-09-14 (debt-dl69 slice) -- `19d97505`; `BDPTStrategyBalanceTest: Passed 72, Failed 0` and `VCMStrategyBalanceTest: Passed 61, Failed 0` (both including a new `schlick_material` topology L) at the time of the fix; on the merge target, with the review's added topology M and DL-125 probe, `BDPTStrategyBalanceTest: Passed 82, Failed 0`, `VCMStrategyBalanceTest: Passed 61, Failed 0`, `SchlickLobePairingTest: Passed 24, Failed 0` | Full record: docs/DL69_BDPT_LOBE_THROUGHPUT.md. **Red-proof (unfixed library, `721e8f12`)**: topology L (schlick_material wall + floor, large area emitter, depth-matched PT reference, 32x32 at 256 spp) measured BDPT mean +19.3686% / p99 +63.0327% / max +981.135% over PT (`BDPTStrategyBalanceTest: Passed 69, Failed 3`) and VCM mean +32.7409% / p99 +76.8255% / max +469.335% (`VCMStrategyBalanceTest: Passed 58, Failed 3`); post-fix BDPT -2.00% and VCM -2.12% on the mean, all three metrics inside the suite bands. Closed-form: `tests/SchlickLobePairingTest.cpp` measures all three arithmetically-available pairings for one selected lobe of a two-lobe overlapping-support `SchlickSPF` against a BRDF quadrature reference Q at 0/30/60 deg -- per-lobe `kray_I/q_I` = 1.000/0.961/0.802 Q, aggregate-over-aggregate `f_agg cos/ISPF::Pdf` = 1.011/0.997/0.999 Q, and the mismatched pairing BDPT used = **1.999/2.000/2.004 Q**, the exact N-times over-count for N=2; the same test measures `(q_I p_I)/ISPF::Pdf()` spanning [0.0035, 1.281] across real draws, red-proving the second half (pdfFwd and pdfRev were two different formulas for one MIS density). **Those closed-form figures were RE-MEASURED 2026-09-14 against the merge target** (master `e290fc64`, which brings DL-67 Slice 0's `SchlickSPF::Pdf` rewrite); the pre-merge readings were 0.999/0.961/0.802, 0.999/1.000/1.001, 1.998/1.999/2.006 and a spread of [0.003, 1.724]. The aggregate estimator itself had to be repaired to survive that merge: it drew its lobe with a FIXED 50/50 coin over the realized container, which is unbiased only while `ISPF::Pdf` IS that fixed mixture, and against Slice 0's true generating density it read 0.842/0.821/0.793 Q (`Passed: 21 Failed: 3`); it now draws with the REAL `RandomlySelect` rule, the procedure `ISPF::Pdf` is the density of. **Per-(s,t) strategy balance** (temporary in-tree instrumentation, since removed; same scene): every s=0 row -- the BSDF-sampled emitter-hit strategy, the one the deflated `pdfFwd` suppressed -- gains MIS weight (t=3 0.3161->0.4144 +31.1%, t=4 0.2262->0.3325 +47.0%, t=5 0.2636->0.3477 +31.9%, t=6 0.1762->0.3165 +79.7%), while row (1,2) -- whose eye endpoint is the FIRST surface vertex, so no multi-lobe scatter enters it -- is unchanged to four decimals as an internal control. **Sibling audit refutes two claims in this row's own original text**: `PolishedSPF` is NOT disjoint -- its dielectric coat lobe is non-delta whenever `scattering` is finite (Phong `< 1e6` / HG `g < 1`) and the diffuse substrate is added whenever `MinValue(Rs) < 1`, both in the same hemisphere, so a glossy `polished_material` over a visible substrate carried the defect; and the row under-enumerated the affected set -- `WardIsotropicGaussianSPF`, `WardAnisotropicEllipticalGaussianSPF`, `IsotropicPhongSPF`, `AshikminShirleyAnisotropicPhongSPF` and `CompositeSPF` all emit overlapping non-delta lobes with per-lobe `.pdf` and were affected too. Confirmed IMMUNE with evidence: `CookTorranceSPF` for GGX's reason (all three lobes set `.pdf = mixPdf`, `CookTorranceSPF.cpp:205/256/316`), `GGXSPF`, `CoatedSPF`, single-lobe SPFs, and `TranslucentSPF` (whose `TranslucentBSDF::value` is a `switch` returning exactly one lobe's term per direction). **Wider consequence, deliberate**: `kray_I` is the SPF's own transport weight and is not in general `f_I cos / p_I` as the paired BSDF evaluates it (TranslucentSPF's carries Beer extinction the BSDF omits; `SchlickSPF`'s Schlick-1994 weight differs from its own BRDF integral by up to 20% at grazing, which is DL-127. A third reason this row used to give -- "the old expression's `cos` used the GEOMETRIC normal while `p_I` is a density around the SHADING normal" -- is WRONG and was struck on review 2026-09-14: `RayIntersectionGeometric::vNormal` IS the shading normal, and `Object::IntersectRay` builds `ri.onb` from it, so the old pairing used one frame, not two), so the fix moves BDPT/VCM/MLT on every material at every non-delta vertex -- always toward PT, which has used `kray` since it shipped. Residuals opened, not fixed: DL-125 (PT's and BDPT's HWSS companion fallback carries the same pattern where `ISPF::EvaluateKrayNM` declines), DL-126 (BDPT/VCM/MLT terminate at a material with no `IBSDF`), DL-127 (`SchlickSPF`'s `kray` vs its own BRDF integral) **MERGE-TARGET RE-MEASUREMENT + CONTROL (review P2-1, 2026-09-14).** On the merge target topology L reads PT 0.0601796 / BDPT 0.0632837 = **+5.16%** (final gate run 0.0601765 / 0.0632883 = +5.17%; `VCMStrategyBalanceTest`'s twin of the same topology reads PT 0.0601639 / VCM 0.0632532 = +5.14%), not the -2.00% quoted above (which was measured pre-merge). The swing is PT's, not BDPT's, and it is attributable: with `src/Library/Materials/SchlickSPF.cpp` alone reverted to its pre-Slice-0 state (isolated build, everything else identical) the same topology reads PT 0.0647011 / BDPT 0.0634197 = -1.98%, i.e. PT moved **-6.99%** and BDPT **-0.21%** when `SchlickSPF::Pdf` changed. `Pdf` enters PT only through its MIS partner arms, and DL-127 (`kray`) was not touched by Slice 0, so the swing is **DL-103's site** -- PT's un-guided escape-side partner is still the SELECTED lobe's own density while its NEE side now uses the true aggregate, so `w_bsdf + w_nee != 1` on a multi-lobe SPF. BDPT is insensitive because DL-69 made its `pdfFwd` and `pdfRev` the SAME function, so the change largely cancels in the ratio chain. A new **topology M** (`BDPTStrategyBalanceTest`) is the discriminator: identical geometry, emitter, camera and both rasterizer strings, with a `ggx_material` wall and a `lambertian_material` floor -- both immune to DL-127 and DL-103 (GGX emits ONE `ScatteredRay` per `Scatter` whose `.pdf` is already the aggregate `mixPdf`; Lambertian is single-lobe) -- and it reads PT 0.0471226 / BDPT 0.0472101 = **+0.19%**, and is unmoved by the same SchlickSPF revert (+0.18%). **Re-quoted 2026-09-17 (round-2 review P2-3/P2-4) against the TRUE merge target**, which brings DL-123 (`GGXBRDF::hemisphericalAlbedo`, which topology M's `ggx_material` wall consumes, and which the `+0.19%` figure above predates): topology M now reads PT 0.0471280 / BDPT 0.0472026 = **+0.16%** (channel-averaged over the render's 3 output channels) -- unchanged within MC noise of the pre-DL-123 figure, so DL-123 does not move this control's conclusion (M stays immune, L's residual stays Schlick-specific). So the residual on L is Schlick-specific, not a generic PT-vs-BDPT asymmetry of this geometry. The suite's standard 8% band is KEPT (2.8 pp of margin). **Per-channel branch premise**: this row's "up to 4x" figure assumes the three per-channel specular rays are three distinct lobes carrying distinct directions -- **DL-101** records that the per-channel loop REUSES one `ScatteredRay` across its lanes, so what actually reaches the container on that branch may be fewer than three; and **DL-127** records that `SchlickSPF`'s per-lobe `kray` is not that lobe's `f_I cos/p_I`, which is why row (a) of the closed-form table reads 0.802 Q at 60 deg rather than 1. **Cost (review P2-5)**: the `pdfFwd` addition makes BDPT evaluate `ISPF::Pdf` one extra time per non-delta vertex per subpath. On an all-`schlick_material` 256x256 / 256-spp BDPT render that is +19.55% user CPU over the pre-DL-69 integrator (163.22 vs 136.53 s, n=16/3); sharing one reconstructed `RayIntersectionGeometric` + `IORStack` between the vertex's two queries (`PathVertexEval::VertexPdfContext`) recovers 4.05% (156.61 s, n=8, Welch t=-6.39), leaving **+14.71%**. The residual is the second `SchlickSPF::Pdf` call itself (~690 ns since Slice 0), not the rebuild. | M | physics-bias | user-visible (BDPT/VCM/MLT renders of any multi-lobe material with overlapping-hemisphere lobes, e.g. `schlick_material`) |

The review's suggested per-lane hoist was implemented and measured at +3.3% (t=1.72) / +3.1% (t=0.72), i.e. no win, and reverted. | ~~DL-100~~ | ~~Found during DL-67 Slice 0 fix pass (debt-slice0, 2026-09-14)~~ | `SchlickSPF`'s (and `WardIsotropicGaussianSPF`'s) specular sampler built its half-vector in the UNFLIPPED `ri.onb`, so on a back-face hit every specular draw was rejected by `Scatter`'s own accept-check and the material silently lost its entire specular lobe | CLOSED 2026-09-17 (debt-dl100 slice) -- `SchlickSampleHalfVector`/`GenerateSpecularRay`/`ComputeSchlickSpecularPdf`/`SchlickInvertSpecular`/`SchlickDiffuseSelectCoefficient` (SchlickSPF.cpp) and `GenerateSpecularRay` (WardIsotropicGaussianSPF.cpp) now consistently build/evaluate the half-vector in the caller's `myonb` (post-FlipW sampling frame) instead of the raw `ri.onb`; `tests/SchlickWardBackfaceEnergyTest.cpp`: `Checks: 19 Failures: 0` (was `10` red, `back=0` on every mirror-image pair) | `SchlickSPF.cpp`'s `GenerateSpecularRay` took an `onb` parameter and never used it: the half-vector was always built from `ri.onb.u()/v()/w()`, while `Scatter`/`ScatterNM` pass the FlipW'd `myonb` and then accept-check against `myonb.w()`. On a back-face hit (`dot(ri.ray.Dir(), ri.onb.w()) > NEARZERO`, i.e. single-sided geometry hit from behind -- double-sided meshes pre-flip `vNormal`, so they never reach it) the two frames differ by sign, the sampled `h` sits in the wrong hemisphere, and `dot(dir, myonb.w()) > 0` fails for every draw. Result: a back-face `schlick_material` hit scattered diffuse-only, at full diffuse weight, with no specular energy at all. **Fix (this slice)**: `SchlickSampleHalfVector` gained an `onb` parameter (threaded from `GenerateSpecularRay`'s own new `onb` parameter, itself fed `myonb` at every one of its 3 call sites in `Scatter`/`ScatterNM`); `ComputeSchlickSpecularPdf` likewise gained an `onb` parameter (fed `myonb` at its 3 call sites plus `SchlickSpecularDensity`'s internal call) and now builds `hdotn`/`hu`/`hv` from it instead of `ri.onb`; `SchlickInvertSpecular` gained the same parameter for its `hz`/`hx`/`hy` projection; `SchlickReplaySpecular` (which already took `myonb`) now actually passes it into `SchlickSampleHalfVector` instead of dropping it; `SchlickDiffuseSelectCoefficient`'s internal quadrature-to-world conversion (`eu`/`ev`/`ew`) switched from `ri.onb.u()/v()/w()` to `myonb.u()/v()/w()`. **Sibling audit** (one-sentence pattern: "the specular half-vector sampler ignores the frame its own accept-check tests"): `WardIsotropicGaussianSPF.cpp`'s `GenerateSpecularRay` carried the IDENTICAL shape of bug -- it already took an `onb` parameter (every call site already passed `myonb`) but the body read `ri.onb.u()/v()/w()` regardless, a dead parameter; FIXED in this slice (one-line body change; `WardIsotropicPdf` needed no change at all, since it recovers `h` from `wi+wo` directly rather than from onb-relative axes, so it was never wrong about the density, only the SAMPLER's frame was). `WardAnisotropicEllipticalGaussianSPF.cpp` audited and found ALREADY CORRECT (its `GenerateSpecularRay` already builds `h` from the passed `onb`, not `ri.onb`). `AshikminShirleyAnisotropicPhongSPF.cpp` carries the same pattern by inspection but is owned by a concurrent sibling "phongpdf" slice per this session's brief -- deliberately left untouched to avoid a duplicate/conflicting edit. `IsotropicPhongSPF`/`CookTorranceSPF`/`GGXSPF`/`CoatedSPF`/`PolishedSPF` audited and confirmed IMMUNE (Phong perturbs an already-correctly-oriented `reflected` vector rather than building `h` from onb axes; the other four select one lobe internally with no separate free function taking a frame). Full derivation, red-proof transcript (10/19 failures pre-fix, all `back=0` against a nonzero mirrored front-face rate), and the sibling-audit table: [DL100_SCHLICK_BACKFACE_SPECULAR.md](DL100_SCHLICK_BACKFACE_SPECULAR.md). | S | energy-loss | user-visible (back-face `schlick_material`/`ward_isotropic_material` hits: specular lobe entirely absent) |
| ~~DL-101~~ | ~~Found during DL-67 Slice 0 fix pass (debt-slice0, 2026-09-14); dynamically confirmed during round-2 review (2026-09-14)~~ | The per-channel specular loop in `SchlickSPF` and both Ward SPFs REUSED one `ScatteredRay` across its three lanes, so a lane whose sampler declined to write a direction pushed the PREVIOUS lane's direction carrying this lane's kray and pdf | CLOSED 2026-09-17 (debt-dl100 slice) -- a fresh `ScatteredRay s;` declared INSIDE the per-channel loop body in all three files; `tests/SchlickWardPerChannelReuseTest.cpp`: `Checks: 4 Failures: 0` (was 3 of 4, `3567/200000`, `58758/200000`, `61708/200000` duplicate-direction calls on the three affected classes; the sanity-control row, whose three per-channel values are EQUAL and so never even enters the per-channel branch, was 0/200000 both pre- and post-fix); `tests/SchlickSPFPdfConsistencyTest.cpp`'s former `DL-101 KNOWN-FAILURE` row is now a real gate, `Checks: 44 Failures: 0` (was 43) | `SchlickSPF::Scatter` declared `ScatteredRay d, s;` once and its per-channel branch called `GenerateSpecularRay(s, ...)` three times in a loop. `GenerateSpecularRay` writes `specular.ray` ONLY when `hdotk > 0`; otherwise the ray is left untouched. On lane 0 that is harmless (a default-constructed `Ray` has direction `(0,0,0)`, so the accept-check rejects it), but on lanes 1 and 2 the untouched ray still holds the PREVIOUS lane's direction -- which generally passes the accept-check -- and the branch then assigns lane `i`'s `kray[i]` and lane `i`'s `ComputeSchlickSpecularPdf(..., rt.v[i], it.v[i])` to it. The container receives a ray at lane `j`'s direction priced as lane `i`. `hdotk <= 0` is reachable for any roughness (the theta warp reaches `pi/2` as `xi -> 0`) and becomes common at grazing incidence with high roughness. Same code shape, verified by reading: `WardIsotropicGaussianSPF::Scatter` and `WardAnisotropicEllipticalGaussianSPF::Scatter` (both conditionally `ray.Set` inside `if(hdotk>0)` and both reuse one `s` across the per-channel loop). NOT present in `IsotropicPhongSPF` (its generator sets the ray unconditionally) or `AshikminShirleyAnisotropicPhongSPF` (its generator returns `false` and the caller gates on it). Slice 0's `Pdf` quadrature models the INTENDED semantics (a lane whose `hdotk <= 0` is rejected), so closing this row removes a modelling gap as well as the sampler bug. Recipe: instrument the per-channel branch to count pushes whose direction is bit-identical to the previous lane's while `hdotk <= 0` for the current lane, on a grazing high-roughness fixture with an `RGBScalarPainter` roughness; fix by declaring the `ScatteredRay` INSIDE the loop (or having `GenerateSpecularRay` return a bool the caller gates on, as `AshikminShirleyAnisotropicPhongSPF` already does) in all three files. **Fix (this slice)**: a fresh `ScatteredRay s;` declared INSIDE the per-channel loop body of `SchlickSPF.cpp`, `WardIsotropicGaussianSPF.cpp` and `WardAnisotropicEllipticalGaussianSPF.cpp` -- confirmed IMMUNE (by reading, this slice): `IsotropicPhongSPF` (its generator sets the ray UNCONDITIONALLY, nothing for a later lane to inherit) and `AshikminShirleyAnisotropicPhongSPF` (its generator returns a `bool` the caller gates on and never touches the `ScatteredRay` argument on a decline). Red-proof (isolated revert of all three files to their pre-DL-100/DL-101 state, three MUTUALLY DISTINCT per-channel values so two genuinely-accepted lanes cannot coincide by chance): `3567/200000` (SchlickSPF), `58758/200000` (WardIsotropic), `61708/200000` (WardAnisotropic) calls with two bit-identical specular directions; `0/200000` post-fix on all three, and on the equal-value sanity control both before and after (that config never enters the per-channel branch at all). `SchlickSPFPdfConsistencyTest`'s own low-then-high-roughness row (a DIFFERENT fixture, matching this row's original discovery config) moved from a non-gated `KNOWN-FAILURE` (TVD 0.01424, over the 0.012 gate) to a real gate at TVD 0.01009, exactly the reduction the design doc predicted. Full derivation: [DL101_PERCHANNEL_SCATTEREDRAY_REUSE.md](DL101_PERCHANNEL_SCATTEREDRAY_REUSE.md). | S | physics-bias | user-visible (`schlick_material`/`ward_*_material` with a per-channel roughness painter, at grazing incidence) |
| ~~DL-103~~ | ~~Found during DL-67 Slice 0 round-2 review (debt-slice0, 2026-09-14)~~ | ~~Slice 0's fix makes `SchlickSPF`'s AGGREGATE `Pdf()`/`PdfNM()` correct, but un-guided default PT's escape-side MIS partner is still the SELECTED lobe's own per-lobe density, not that aggregate -- so `w_bsdf + w_nee != 1` at a Schlick vertex even after Slice 0, in ordinary (non-guided) PT~~ | CLOSED 2026-09-17 (debt-dl103 slice) -- fix `86f4c623`, red-proof `0535bfb6`, band tightening `b5ddb691`; `PTGuidingMISPartitionTest: 83 passed, 0 failed` (red 81/2), `BDPTStrategyBalanceTest: Passed 93, Failed 0` and `VCMStrategyBalanceTest: Passed 68, Failed 0` both with topology L's mean band tightened 8% -> 2% | `LightSampler.cpp`'s NEE arms weight against `pMaterial->Pdf(...)` (`LightSampler.cpp:2545,2708`, `PdfNM` twins `:3121,3244`) -- the AGGREGATE density Slice 0 fixed. But `PathTracingIntegrator.cpp` PART 3's escape-side partner, with guiding inactive, is `misBsdfPdf = effectiveBsdfPdf` (`PathTracingIntegrator.cpp:3836`), stored as `rs2.bsdfPdf = effectiveBsdfPdf; rs2.bsdfMisPdf = misBsdfPdf;` (`:3867-3868`), where `effectiveBsdfPdf = pS->isDelta ? 0 : pS->pdf` (`:3521`) -- the SELECTED lobe's own per-lobe density, not the material's aggregate `ISPF::Pdf()`. At a single-lobe SPF the two coincide, so this is invisible there; at a multi-lobe SPF like Schlick they are different quantities, so the NEE/escape MIS pair does not partition to one even after Slice 0's fix. This is a DIFFERENT instance than DL-67 (DL-67 is scoped to the GUIDED one-sample/RIS candidate branches, `PathTracingIntegrator.cpp` PART 3's guided block and `BDPTIntegrator.cpp`'s eye/light RIS; this row is the UN-GUIDED default-PT escape/NEE pair, which DL-67's own text does not name) and a different mechanism than DL-69 (BDPT/VCM ordinary throughput). Measured (sample-weighted `w_bsdf+w_nee` across three Schlick-material furnace configurations, un-guided default PT): 1.0102 / 1.0627 / 1.0667 at this slice's HEAD vs 1.0530 / 1.0735 / 1.0697 on pre-slice master `32824325` -- Slice 0's fix IMPROVES the partition (the aggregate it corrected is closer to the per-lobe density than the old, more wrongly-weighted aggregate was) but does not close it; worst single directional bin measured 1.353. Recipe: make the escape side's `misBsdfPdf`/`bsdfMisPdf` the material's AGGREGATE `Pdf()` (not the selected lobe's `pS->pdf`) when guiding is inactive -- the DL-74 two-roles design (`bsdfPdf` vs `bsdfMisPdf`) already separates the two fields, so this is a matter of what value populates `bsdfMisPdf` in the un-guided branch, not new machinery; the same fix should be checked against every other multi-emit SPF DL-67's own row lists (`PolishedSPF`/`PolishedBRDF`, `TranslucentSPF`, `CompositeSPF`, `BioSpecSkinSPF`, `GenericHumanTissueSPF`). Red-proof: a `PTGuidingMISPartitionTest`-style closed-form furnace on `schlick_material` with guiding OFF, gating `w_bsdf+w_nee` to 1 within MC noise (the existing `PTGuidingMISPartitionTest` only exercises GUIDED configurations, so it does not see this row). Cross-references: DL-67 (guided PT/BDPT candidates, still open), DL-69 (BDPT/VCM ordinary throughput). See [DL67_SLICE0_SCHLICK_PDF_WEIGHTS.md](DL67_SLICE0_SCHLICK_PDF_WEIGHTS.md) SS5. **Render-level evidence added (round-2 review P2-3/P2-4, 2026-09-17):** `tests/BDPTStrategyBalanceTest.cpp` topology L (`TestSchlickMultiLobe`, DL-69's own multi-lobe `schlick_material` wall+floor scene) is this row's mechanism made concrete at full-render scale, not just a furnace. The DL-69 row's own isolated one-file A/B (`src/Library/Materials/SchlickSPF.cpp` alone reverted to its pre-Slice-0 state, everything else identical, same topology re-rendered) measured PT move -6.99% (0.0647011 -> 0.0601765) against BDPT's -0.21% (0.0634197 -> 0.0632883) when `SchlickSPF::Pdf` changed. BDPT's `pdfFwd`/`pdfRev` are the SAME aggregate function since DL-69, so BDPT is close to insensitive to the Pdf rewrite; PT's un-guided escape-side partner is still the selected lobe's density (this row's defect), so PT is NOT insensitive -- a 7% render-level swing from touching only the density Slice 0 fixed. **This resizes the row from S-M to M**: it is not only a furnace-level MIS-weight defect but a measured double-digit-percent driver of topology L's PT reference in a real regression suite. **Consequence for the suite's gate**: `BDPTStrategyBalanceTest`/`VCMStrategyBalanceTest` topology L's 8% band is marked PROVISIONAL pending this row -- PT is not an integrator-free reference for a `schlick_material` scene while this row is open (there is no closed form or furnace for topology L's full multi-bounce geometry, so an integrator-free reference is not currently available; see the topology's own comment in `BDPTStrategyBalanceTest.cpp`). A pass on topology L is not evidence BDPT is correct to the quoted percentage, only that BDPT and (a possibly biased) PT agree to within that band. **CLOSURE (2026-09-17, debt-dl103 slice; branched from `master` `aa64c45e`, BEFORE `debt-dl127` merged as `19944c34` -- every "DL-127 is still open" caveat below is true at that base, and every A/B here is an isolated `PathTracingIntegrator.cpp`-only revert with `SchlickSPF` identical on both sides, so the attributions stand; but topology L's ABSOLUTE means will move on the merged tree because `kray` changed, so re-run both balance suites after the merge and re-quote them -- the tightened band is on the RATIO, which is the part that should survive).** Full record: [DL103_PT_ESCAPE_MIS_PARTNER.md](DL103_PT_ESCAPE_MIS_PARTNER.md). Both PART 3 sites in `PathTracingIntegrator.cpp` (the RGB/NM templated body and the HWSS body -- the latter's own comment used to claim "no partition mismatch exists here" on the FALSE premise that the NEE arms use the raw material pdf) now populate `RAY_STATE::bsdfMisPdf` from the aggregate `ISPF::Pdf()`/`PdfNM(..., heroNM, ...)` evaluated for the FINAL traced direction under the LIVE `iorStack`; `bsdfPdf` is untouched (DL-74's two-fields design, applied, not extended). The guided branch was already correct (`PTGuidingMisPdf::Eval` has blended the AGGREGATE since DL-74) and is byte-identical. **One guard, and it is not cosmetic**: where the aggregate reads 0 at a direction the technique really did generate (DL-41 -- `TranslucentSPF`'s two Phong `cos^N` lobes), the guiding-INACTIVE branch falls back to the lobe's own density, because handing 0 to BOTH sides means "no partner exists" on both (`w_bsdf = 1` AND `w_nee = 1`), a full double count strictly worse than the pre-DL-103 asymmetry; BDPT's DL-69 fix applies the same rule at the same place (the thresholds differ harmlessly -- PT tests `> 0`, BDPT `> NEARZERO`, which can only diverge for an aggregate density in `(0, 1e-12]`). **Red-proof** (`tests/PTGuidingMISPartitionTest.cpp`, two new closed-form furnace rows on a synthetic two-lobe SPF whose lobes carry CONSTANT `kray` -- so `RandomlySelect`'s choice is exactly `c_I` and the aggregate is a well-defined mixture -- and whose BRDF is `Pdf(w)/cos` so the bihemispherical albedo is exactly 1): env furnace `(j)` measured 0.666203 against the closed form 0.6, `relErr=11.0338%`; area furnace `(k)` 0.280529 against 0.248936, `relErr=12.6912%`; `81 passed, 2 failed`. Post-fix 0.599399 (`0.100175%`) and 0.248949 (`0.00517102%`), `83 passed, 0 failed`. Each row carries a SINGLE-lobe CONTROL -- the identical BRDF and identical marginal sampling density emitted as ONE `ScatteredRay` instead of two -- green before AND after (0.146% / 0.120%), which is what proves the rows measure the per-lobe/aggregate partner mismatch and not the harness. A deterministic PREMISE on PRODUCTION code ties the synthetic material to measured behaviour: over 4096 `Scatter` calls at oblique incidence, `SchlickSPF`'s aggregate/selected-lobe pdf ratio spans `[0.505783, 10.8666]` and `IsotropicPhongSPF`'s `[0.5, 38.686]`. Both pre-fix errors match an independent quadrature of the two weightings over their domain (+11.2% predicted for (j) against the env sampler's `1/4pi`; +12.70% for (k)) -- and the SAME quadrature predicts **-0.45%** for the R=2 emitter the DL-74 area row uses, against a measured -0.33%, which is why (k) needed its own smaller (R=0.5) emitter: a partition defect is only visible where the two techniques' densities are COMPARABLE. **Render-level, and this row's own headline claim discharged**: isolated A/B on `PathTracingIntegrator.cpp` alone, topology L, n=4 per side -- BDPT pre-fix PT `0.0601656 +/- 0.0000054` / BDPT `0.0632933 +/- 0.0000024` = **+5.198% +/- 0.011 pp**, post-fix PT `0.0632676 +/- 0.0000110` / BDPT `0.0632930 +/- 0.0000044` = **+0.040% +/- 0.012 pp**; VCM pre-fix +5.119% +/- 0.020 pp, post-fix -0.026% +/- 0.011 pp. **PT moved +5.16%/+5.14%; BDPT and VCM moved -0.0005%/-0.002%** -- the only file that changed lives in PT, and BDPT's `pdfFwd` (and VCM's, which derives from it) has been the aggregate since DL-69. Topology M (the immune GGX+Lambertian control) is unmoved at +0.18% against its pre-slice +0.16%. The 8% band's **PROVISIONAL marker is removed** and the MEAN band tightened to **2%** in both suites (~115x the measured run-to-run sigma; catches a DL-103-class 5.2% and a DL-69-class 19% regression); p99/max stay at the shared 0.25/1.00. What a pass there now claims is "PT and BDPT/VCM agree to 2% on a multi-lobe material" -- NOT that either is absolutely correct, since DL-127 is still open and BOTH integrators consume `kray`, so a residual from that row sits inside the agreement rather than showing as a gap. **Cost**: one extra aggregate `ISPF::Pdf()` per non-delta vertex at EVERY material. Interleaved pre/post A/B with two separately built binaries on `scenes/Tests/BDPT/cornellbox_bdpt_materials_pt.RISEscene` (512x512, PT, 128 spp), n=6 pairs, user CPU: pre `228.16 +/- 3.85 s`, post `232.99 +/- 2.23 s`, paired delta `+4.82 +/- 3.49 s` = **+2.11%**, paired t = 3.39. (A first non-interleaved batch read +3.09%, Welch t = 2.90; the interleaved pairing is the number to quote.) **That scene has ONE `schlick_material` among twelve receivers, so it is the REALISTIC number, not a bound (review P2-2)**: on an ALL-`schlick_material` scene (topology L's own wall+floor at 320x320/256spp, depth 5, same interleaved protocol, n=6) the same A/B reads `80.04 +/- 1.22 s` -> `114.66 +/- 0.85 s` = **+34.61 +/- 1.19 s = +43.25%** (paired t = 71.1), against an all-`lambertian_material` control differing by exactly one chunk at `+2.50%` (t = 1.75, not significant) -- i.e. the cost is essentially the `SchlickSPF::Pdf` call itself, once per non-delta bounce. **Deferred evaluation was measured and DECLINED.** Temporary producer/consumer counters (since reverted) show the partner is consumed on only `1035858 / 2078282` = 50.2% of evaluations on the all-Schlick scene and `1950141 / 125044482` = 1.6% on the mixed cornell one; a `variant X` build that performs the full capture a lazy consumer would need (`RayIntersectionGeometric` + live `IORStack` + direction + `ISPF*`) and does NOT call `Pdf` costs `85.69 +/- 1.42 s` against pre-fix `84.16 +/- 3.29 s` (**+1.8%**) and eager `120.09 +/- 2.61 s`, so a lazy design projects to `+23.1%` instead of `+42.7%` -- about 46% of the added cost recovered on the worst case and nearly all of it on the realistic one. It is declined on STRUCTURE, not arithmetic: `sizeof(RAY_STATE)` is 96 bytes and is copied per bounce AND per shadow ray while `sizeof(RayIntersectionGeometric)` is 1120 (a ~13x growth of the hottest copied struct), so the capture cannot live in `RAY_STATE`; but `RayCasterEnvEscapeMISWeight` and `EmissionShaderOp` read `MisPartnerPdf()` THROUGH `RAY_STATE` with no access to the producing vertex, and the entry vertex has no producing vertex at all -- so a deferred design is a THREE-case partner computation, which is the exact shape DL-74's own postmortem blames for this bug family, and it re-introduces the argument-reconstruction hazard DL-74 P2 measured at -14% (the IOR-stack sentinel) by construction (`RayIntersectionGeometric` is additionally NOT trivially copyable -- it holds a polymorphic `OrthonormalBasis3D` -- so variant X's +1.8% is a lower bound). **The better direction, recorded for a future slice**: a multi-emit SPF already knows its mixture weights at `Scatter` time and could stamp the aggregate density on each emitted `ScatteredRay` at near-zero marginal cost -- same function, same arguments, no capture and no second code path -- which is exactly what `GGXSPF`/`CookTorranceSPF` already do and why they are immune to DL-69 and DL-103 at all. So the evaluation is unconditional, which also keeps the two sides of the pair structurally identical. **Sibling audit**: the guided/RIS branches, BDPT's `pdfFwd` (DL-69), VCM's `EvaluateS0Impl`/`EvaluateNEEImpl` and dVCM recurrence, MLT (shares BDPT's generators), `RayCasterEnvEscapeMISWeight` (a consumer of `MisPartnerPdf()`, not a producer), `RayCaster.cpp`'s two volume continuations (single-lobe phase, matched by `MediumScatterMaterial::Pdf` -- DL-73's ruling), PT's BSSRDF exit continuations (paired with `BSSRDFEntryMaterial::Pdf`, literally `cosTheta*INV_PI`) and PT's no-BRDF continuation (`rs2.bsdfPdf` is ALWAYS 0 there -- Dielectric/PerfectReflector/PerfectRefractor emit only `isDelta` lobes, and BioSpecSkin/GenericHumanTissue never assign `.pdf` at all so it keeps `ScatteredRay()`'s 0, their `ISPF::Pdf` being the base-class 0 as well) were each confirmed IMMUNE or already-fixed with evidence. **Interaction with DL-125 (review P2-1), recorded not fixed**: this fix RAISES the weight the BSDF-sampling strategy carries at a multi-lobe vertex, and on the HWSS path that strategy's companion throughput is already inflated by DL-125's aggregate-over-per-lobe fallback -- so it amplifies that open row. Measured PT-vs-PT on topology L (n=3/side): PT pel +5.134%, PT spectral hero-only +5.202% (the same correction landing identically), PT spectral `hwss TRUE` **+12.534%**, ratio hwssTRUE/pel `1.5213 +/- 0.0084` -> `1.6284 +/- 0.0069`; the immune topology-M control is unchanged to 0.014% on all three rows. DL-125's row is upgraded to a MEASURED row and `tests/BDPTStrategyBalanceTest.cpp` gains `TestPTSpectralHWSSKnownDefect` (+ control) pinning 1.63 as a KNOWN-DEFECT band for its closure to move. Two DISTINCT defects found and opened rather than fixed: **DL-170** (HWSS carries ONE scalar MIS partner across all `SampledWavelengths::N` companion wavelengths while each companion's NEE arm evaluates `PdfNM` at its own lambda) and **DL-171** (the legacy shader-op chain never populates `RAY_STATE::bsdfPdf`/`bsdfMisPdf` at all, so `EmissionShaderOp` takes weight 1 while `DirectLightingShaderOp`'s NEE arm still weights against a positive aggregate pdf -- an ABSENT partner, not a wrong one). **Merged-tree re-quote (master c1a26cc2, i.e. with DL-127's kray change, gate run 2026-09-18):** topology L PT 0.0642577 / BDPT 0.0642817 = **+0.037 %**; the GGX/Lambertian control PT 0.0471354 / BDPT 0.0472093 = +0.157 % -- matching the dl127 review's scratch combination (1.00008). The absolute level moved from ~0.0633 to ~0.0643 (DL-127), the ratio did not. | M | physics-bias | user-visible (default, un-guided PT renders of `schlick_material` and any other multi-lobe, multi-emit material; NEE partner MIS weight; drives a measured 7% PT-side swing on `BDPTStrategyBalanceTest` topology L) |
| ~~DL-102~~ | ~~Found during DL-67 Slice 0 fix pass (debt-slice0, 2026-09-14)~~ CLOSED 2026-09-14 — `eb328217`, `SchlickSPFPdfConsistencyTest` `Checks: 34 Failures: 0` | ~~`ComputeSchlickSpecularPdf`'s azimuthal density for `isotropy != 1` was a DIFFERENT DISTRIBUTION from the one `GenerateSpecularRay` draws -- not a mis-scaled version of it~~ The density now matches the sampler's own warp | CLOSED-by-test | The helper returned `p/(2 pi (cos^2 phi + p^2 sin^2 phi))`, with a comment asserting it was "the phi PDF (from the sampling inversion)". The sampler draws, per quadrant, `phi = (pi/2) sqrt(p^2 v^2/(1 - v^2 + v^2 p^2))` with `v ~ U[0,1)`; inverting, `v = t/sqrt(p^2 + t^2(1-p^2))` with `t := phi/(pi/2)` measured from the quadrant's own axis, so the true density is `p^2/(2 pi (p^2 + t^2(1-p^2))^(3/2))`. Both forms integrate to 1 over `[0,2pi)` and both agree at `p=1`, which is how it survived. Against a 2 000 000-draw histogram of the real sampler the shipped form was off by up to 25x (`p=0.3`, `phi=1.702`: 0.4526 vs 0.0181 true; `p=0.6`, same `phi`: 0.2575 vs 0.0674); the corrected form matches the histogram to MC noise at `p` = 1, 0.8, 0.6 and 0.3. Gated by `SchlickSPFPdfConsistencyTest`'s anisotropic rows (isotropy 0.6 and 0.8) via both the normalisation and total-variation gates. Filed as its own row rather than folded into DL-67 because it is a different bug with a different mechanism, and a future reader looking for "why did anisotropic Schlick MIS weights change" needs to find it. | S | physics-bias | user-visible (`schlick_material` with `isotropy != 1`: NEE/BDPT/VCM MIS weights) |
| ~~DL-125~~ | ~~Found during DL-69's sibling audit (debt-dl69 slice, 2026-09-14)~~ | ~~PT's and BDPT's HWSS COMPANION-wavelength throughput falls back to an aggregate-BSDF-over-per-lobe-pdf pairing whenever the SPF does not implement `ISPF::EvaluateKrayNM`~~ | **CLOSED 2026-09-19** (implementation history: 2026-09-18) -- `f60ac4ad` (the five SPFs) + `f0a5c75e` (`RecomputeSubpathThroughputNM`) + `7e841e85` (band retirement). `SchlickSPF`, `WardIsotropicGaussianSPF`, `WardAnisotropicEllipticalGaussianSPF`, `IsotropicPhongSPF` and `AshikminShirleyAnisotropicPhongSPF` now implement `ISPF::EvaluateKrayNM`. **PT probe closed decisively**: `BDPTStrategyBalanceTest`'s PT-pel-vs-PT-spectral-`hwss TRUE` ratio on topology L moved `1.60711 +/- 0.00433` -> `1.00093 +/- 0.00043` (historical isolated A/B on the five SPF .cpp/.h pairs alone, n = 3 per side, library AND test target rebuilt on each side), with the immune topology-M control unchanged (`0.99962` -> `1.00000`); the KNOWN-DEFECT band `1.35 .. 1.95` is RETIRED and replaced by a two-sided parity gate `0.97 .. 1.03` (`TestPTSpectralHWSSKnownDefect` renamed `TestPTSpectralHWSSCompanionParity`). **A THIRD ladder the row did not name was found and fixed**: the two `hwssBetaNM` loops in `BDPTIntegrator.cpp`'s subpath generators are read only for Russian roulette and guiding training (DL-126 review round 2), so the render-visible companion pricing for BDPT, VCM and MLT is `BDPTIntegrator::RecomputeSubpathThroughputNM`, which rescaled by the AGGREGATE ratio `f_agg(lambda_c)/f_agg(lambda_h)`; it now asks the SPF for the selected lobe's own kray ratio (new `BDPTVertex::scatterType`, left `eRayUnknown` when guiding SUBSTITUTED the direction so hero and companion stay on the same estimator). **The render-level half took THREE rounds to characterise correctly and the final answer is that it is a VARIANCE change, not a mean change.**  Round 1 reported an achromatic move of `-0.906% -> -1.437%` (n = 8, Welch t = -4.43) as a measured systematic and DERIVED a gate band from it; round 2 refuted that and the re-measurement is unambiguous.  **(i) The achromatic move is a LOW-SPP ARTIFACT.**  hwssTRUE-ONLY, pre/post interleaved, at `num_wavelengths 160` (see (ii)), on topology P: post/pre = `0.99484` (t = -5.67) at 256 spp, `1.00077` (t = +1.49) at 512, `0.99990` (t = -0.16) at 1024, `0.99979` (t = -0.34) at 2048 -- the two builds converge to the SAME mean, so nothing is biased.  What genuinely changed is the TAIL, and the mechanism is structural: `lobeRatio = krayComp/krayHero` is UNBOUNDED as `krayHero -> 0`, while the aggregate ratio it replaced was a convex blend bounded between the two lobes' own ratios; the post-fix image's run-to-run sd correspondingly stops falling cleanly with sample count (`0.320 / 0.123 / 0.193 / 0.145 %` relative at 256 / 512 / 1024 / 2048).  Measured tail, historical isolated A/B, n = 3 per side, BDPT `hwss TRUE` at 256 spp: **p99 +33.4 / +13.2 / +24.9 % (R/G/B) and max +19.4 / +15.0 / +81.5 %, with the MEANS unmoved (-0.8 / 0.0 / -0.8 %)**.  **The measured population does not justify clipping the companion ratios**; it does not prove that every alternative formulation is unbounded: `HWSSCompanionKrayTest` section E's synthetic-intersection ratio characterisation (0/35/70 deg, not a render-weighted tail distribution or a bound on extreme-grazing heroes) finds that of 1969 ratios above 4x on this material, **ZERO have `krayHero < 1e-3`** -- the worst is 7.40x at `krayHero = 0.127`, and the smallest hero weight seen at all is 0.083 -- so these sampled heavy ratios are legitimate spectral swings rather than a numerical near-zero tail. This finite synthetic population establishes neither an extreme-grazing bound nor the impossibility of other reformulations; see DL69 section 6.3 for the scope.  **(ii) There is NO material-independent HWSS offset; round 1's claim of one was wrong, and so was using topology M as the reference.**  `SpectralConfig::numWavelengths` defaults to 10 and `BDPTSpectralRasterizer.cpp`'s `hwss FALSE` branch samples a DISCRETE `lambda_begin + int(xi*num_wavelengths)*wavelength_steps` LEFT-ENDPOINT grid, while `hwss TRUE` calls `SampledWavelengths::SampleEquidistant`, which is continuous-stratified and never consults `num_wavelengths` (both verified in source) -- so at the default the two branches estimate DIFFERENT INTEGRALS and every hwssTRUE/hwssFALSE comparison carries ~-1% of pure quadrature difference.  Measured (post-fix, n = 6): topology L achromatic `-1.028% / +0.128% / +0.047%` at `num_wavelengths` 10 / 40 / 160.  Topology P's four renders are now pinned at `num_wavelengths 160`.  **(iii) The B/R pre-vs-post delta is NOT separable in either direction**: four independent measurements read `+0.335 pp` (t = 0.97), `+0.079 pp` (t = 0.27, n = 12 at nw 160) and the relayed review's `-0.075 pp` (t = -0.25) and `+0.189 pp` (t = 1.36) -- these historical point estimates span **-0.075 to +0.335 pp** and resolve neither the sign nor a hard bound on the true delta; the former `0.2 pp` bound and "directionally consistent" claim are withdrawn.  The B/R LEVEL is itself sample-count-dependent (a ratio-of-ratios bias from the noisy small-R denominator), so no absolute B/R at one spp is a stable quantity either.  **What survives from round 1**: the `lobeRatio *= 4.0` sensitivity control (achromatic `0.986 -> 3.163`, B/R `-19.8 pp`, so the branch is far from inert), the CANCELLATION explanation for why the mean barely moves, and the topology-L attribution control (post/pre `0.998..1.002` per channel, so the change is confined to divergent lobe spectra).  Two candidate separating topologies asymmetric in MAGNITUDE as well as hue (`0.02 0.02 0.80`/`0.90 0.60 0.05`, `0.80 0.75 0.70`/`0.05 0.10 0.60`) were screened and did NOT separate better than P; the recipe is in the closure doc.  **Topology P is now a FOUR-ESTIMATOR probe** (PT spectral hwss FALSE / TRUE, BDPT spectral hwss FALSE / TRUE, all at nw 160): the three that agree are gated to parity at 1.5%, and BDPT `hwss TRUE`'s B/R outlier (matched-spp n/sd calibration at two spp levels in DL69 §6.3) is pinned as **DL-219**, a PRE-EXISTING defect this row neither created nor closes (historical isolated A/B at nw 160, n = 12: `-4.130%` pre vs `-4.051%` post, paired delta `+0.079 pp`, t = 0.27).  `CompositeSPF` remains DL-221. Translucent selected-lobe evaluation and incoming-distance-preserving companion replay now close DL-222 (2026-09-19); ordinary connection reconstruction remains DL-223. Unsupported Translucent types retain the diagnostic identity. **Two residual rows are recorded, not changed: DL-216 and DL-219.**  DL-219 is the four-estimator finding above.  **DL-216** -- every override divides by `p_I(lambda_c)` while the direction was drawn from `p_I(lambda_h)`, which coincide only where the lobe DENSITY is wavelength-independent (every reflectance-only chromatic material, i.e. what all of this row's measurements exercise) and differ under a chromatic SHAPE painter | `tests/HWSSCompanionKrayTest.cpp`: integrated **169/0**, replay-distance red **153/16** at `a90899b1`. Sections: A/A2 `EvaluateKrayNM` reproduces `ScatterNM`'s own `krayNM` at the same wavelength to `<= 1.24e-14` on all five classes, with both wavelength-INDEPENDENT and wavelength-VARYING shape painters (pre-fix: 0 checked, 5992-7183 DECLINED per class); B the same at a COMPANION wavelength queried at the HERO's direction, `<= 1.41e-14`, with `dir drift 0` (the two identically-seeded samplers provably drew the same directions) and a hero-vs-companion spread of 0.62-0.89 (so the cross-wavelength check is not vacuous); C `EvaluateKrayNM * p_lobe == f_lobe cos` through the material's own `IBSDF::valueNM`, `<= 2.73e-14` on every specular row; D negative controls (`GGXSPF` and `LambertianSPF` must keep DECLINING, an unsupported `rayType` must decline, and BOTH `CompositeSPF` and `TranslucentSPF` must NAME themselves through the new `ISPF::PerLobeDensityFallbackName()`); E the DL-221/DL-222 premise above. A one-shot-per-class warning (`RISE::NotePerLobeDensityCompanionFallback`, `ScatteredRayContainer.cpp`) fires at all three ladders when a self-naming SPF takes the fallback. **Cost** (interleaved separately-built `bin/rise` binaries): on an all-`schlick_material` 128x128 scene (a worst case, every receiver multi-lobe), PT spectral `hwss TRUE` user CPU 55.92s -> 56.84s (**+1.65%**, n = 3, paired t = 4.1) and BDPT spectral `hwss TRUE` 34.04s -> 35.07s (**+3.02%**, n = 3, paired t = 3.3); the suite is 141/0 on the PRE-fix library too, which is what makes DL-219's pinned row a statement about the scene rather than about this fix), `VCMStrategyBalanceTest` 74/0, `MLTSpectralHWSSNormalizationTest` 8/0, `VCMSpectralRecurrenceTest` 38/0, `VCMEyePostPassTest` 31/0, `VCMLightPostPassTest` 34/0, `PTGuidingMISPartitionTest` 98/0, `OptimalMISTrainingSitesTest` 111/0, `EnvLightBalanceTest` 123/0, `SchlickKrayBRDFConsistencyTest` 557/0, `WardDensityKrayTest` 352/0, `SPFBSDFConsistencyTest` all pass, `SPFPdfConsistencyTest` all pass, `SchlickSPFPdfConsistencyTest` 44/0, `AshikminShirleySPFPdfConsistencyTest` 99/0, `IsotropicPhongSPFPdfConsistencyTest` 46/0, `SchlickWardBackfaceEnergyTest` 19/0, `SchlickWardPerChannelReuseTest` 4/0, `SchlickLobePairingTest` 27/0, `BDPTVertexRIGRebuildTest` 68/0, `PathValueOpsTest` all pass, `CompositeExtinctionTest` all pass, `CookTorranceHWSSTest` 7/0, `LegacyChainMISPartnerTest` 20/0, `BDPTPhantomStrategyWeightTest` all pass, `CstDeriveGoldenTest` 452 MATCH / 0 DRIFT, `SourceHygieneTest` 167/0. **Sibling audit, REFUTED with evidence**: PT's HWSS body never reaches the companion ladder for a material with no `IBSDF` at all (`PathTracingIntegrator.cpp`'s `if( !pBRDFCur )` block DELEGATES every live wavelength to `IntegrateFromHitNM` and breaks), so a `dielectric_material` / `perfectreflector_material` vertex does NOT zero its companions -- confirmed by render: a mirror scene reads RMSE `3.70e-3` for `hwss TRUE` against the `pathtracing_pel_rasterizer` reference, TIGHTER than hero-only's `1.67e-2`. DL-69's own doc had this backwards too and was corrected in the same slice. **A second, independent instance of the same bug pattern was found by testing, one layer up in VCM's own MIS recurrence**: `VCMIntegrator.cpp`'s `ConvertLightSubpath`/`ConvertEyeSubpath` branch on `v.isDelta` alone to decide whether a vertex's outgoing update needs a finite-density BSDF-sampling formula or the opaque/specular one; a null-BSDF, non-delta vertex took the density-requiring branch, silently no-op'd on the (correctly zero) `next.pdfFwd`, and left the running dVCM/dVC/dVM state frozen -- measured to starve VCM's own S0 strategy down to 1.6% of PT even after BDPT's continuation fix alone. Fixed by routing `v.isDelta \|\| !v.isConnectible` through the specular branch in both functions. This edit was outside this slice's originally-scoped touch set (`BDPTIntegrator.cpp`/`PathVertexEval`/materials) but was made anyway because it is the same audited bug pattern and this row's own success criterion (VCM/PT -> 1) is unreachable without it; it does not touch either of the two files reserved for concurrent slices. **Sibling audit** (docs/DL126_BDPT_NULL_BSDF_CONTINUATION.md §4): the only two affected materials are `biospec_skin_material` and `generic_human_tissue_material` (every other null-`GetBSDF()` material -- Dielectric, PerfectReflector, PerfectRefractor -- is pure-delta and already immune; `NullMaterial`'s base default has no SPF either, moot); `VCMIntegrator::EvaluateMerges`, the medium-vertex `connectible` derivation, the HWSS hero/companion ratio helper, and `HasDispersiveDeltaVertex` were all audited and found ALREADY correctly keyed on `isConnectible`/`GetBSDF()` rather than `isDelta` -- no changes needed there. MLT sanity: a direct `mlt_rasterizer` bootstrap on the red-proof scene went from a hard "Bootstrap found zero luminance" pre-fix to a normal bootstrap (mean luminance 0.0678) post-fix, with zero code changes to `MLTRasterizer.cpp` (it constructs a `BDPTIntegrator` directly). Gate (clean rebuild, zero warnings): `MISWeightsTest` 59/0, `BDPTVertexRIGRebuildTest` 68/0, `BDPTPhantomStrategyWeightTest` all passed, `ConnectionLegalityTest` 316/0, `VCMEyePostPassTest` 31/0, `VCMLightPostPassTest` 34/0, `PSSMLTStreamAliasingTest` all passed, `SSSRadianceScalingTest` 574017/0 (unchanged baseline), `CstDeriveGoldenTest` 452 MATCH/0 DRIFT, `SourceHygieneTest` 165/0. **New debt opened**: DL-183 (a distinct, unrelated bug found while building this row's red-proof scene -- `GenericHumanTissueSPF::Scatter`/`ScatterNM` set the scattered ray's origin to `ri.ray.origin` instead of `ri.ptIntersection`, unlike the sibling `BioSpecSkinSPF`, which does it correctly -- NOT fixed here, different bug pattern). **REVIEW ROUND 2 (2026-09-18): confirmed the MIS ruling and VCM-recurrence fix independently (re-derived the (s,t) enumeration; no regression on any other topology) and found one real P1 in round 1's own HWSS handling plus four smaller corrections -- full account in docs/DL126_BDPT_NULL_BSDF_CONTINUATION.md §7.** P1: round 1's `nullBSDFContinuation` branch scaled every HWSS companion wavelength by the hero's own `krayNM`, copying the DELTA branch's convention without checking it applies -- it does not (a delta lobe IS wavelength-independent; this material's colour IS its wavelength-dependent response), and that edit site (`hwssBetaNM`) turned out to be read only for Russian-Roulette/training, never the rendered image, so it was inert for the actual bug either way. Measured (review): hwss=TRUE achromatic ratio 1.33x / blue channel 3.8x PT, grey-ified. The REAL companion pricing site is `BDPTIntegrator::RecomputeSubpathThroughputNM` (called per companion wavelength by `BDPTSpectralRasterizer.cpp`/`VCMSpectralRasterizer.cpp`/`MLTSpectralRasterizer.cpp`), whose own Phase-3 ratio computation had the identical `GetBSDF()`-gated pattern and fell through to "ratio = 1.0" for a null-BSDF vertex. Fixed there instead: a null-BSDF vertex now zeroes the companion's contribution (mirroring PT's own HWSS companion fallback), and a new static `BDPTIntegrator::HasNullBSDFContinuationVertex` lets all three spectral rasterizers `swl.TerminateSecondary()` up front so the zeroed companions are excluded from the sample-count denominator (avoiding the exact dilution bias the file's pre-existing dispersive-termination comment warns about). Post-fix topology N: achromatic ratio 1.00 (was 1.33), B/R ratio 0.225 vs a 0.234 reference (was 1.09 vs 0.24). `BDPTStrategyBalanceTest` 104/0 (new `TestNullBSDFHWSSCompanionLadder`), `VCMStrategyBalanceTest` 74/0. Four corrections: (1) a medium-vertex `isConnectible` asymmetry between light-rooted and eye-rooted derivations was investigated and, on analysis, judged NOT a partition defect -- **WRONG, corrected in review round 4 (P2-3): the reasoning conflated "one vertex array's own self-consistency" with the actual object of a partition-of-unity claim, which is about different STRATEGIES/samples, not one array. Rewritten and filed as DL-200 (reachability not yet demonstrated by either party's testing) -- see docs/DEBT_LEDGER.md's DL-200 row and the doc's rewritten §7.2.**; (2) the stale "PositiveMagnitude(f) <= 0 gate" comment (both in `BDPTIntegrator.cpp` and `CLAUDE.md`'s DL-69 bullet) was corrected; (3) `MISWeight`'s own new comment overclaimed the skipped vertex's `pdfFwd`/`pdfRev` are "often exactly 1" -- corrected: they are generically NONZERO (from the neighbouring ordinary vertices), and the skip is required regardless of their value; (4) topology N's p99 metric was quantised in coarse steps at 32 spp (a coin-flip band by construction) -- moved to a dedicated 256-spp rasterizer pair, and the pre-fix red-proof's own max metric is now correctly described as never having been red (a relative diff of exactly 1.0, at the 100% band boundary, not beyond it). the row's earlier 0.99927/0.96142/0.80200 figures were from before this branch merged master), while the aggregate-over-aggregate pairing reads 1.01051 / 0.99724 / 0.99914 -- i.e. the deficit is entirely in the SPF's `kray` convention, not in the estimator (the aggregate pairing's own small departure from 1.0 is DL-69's known ~1.1% `SchlickSPF::Pdf` residual, not this row's `kray` defect -- see `SchlickLobePairingTest.cpp`'s replicated measurement). **CLOSURE (2026-09-18, docs/DL127_SCHLICK_KRAY_VS_BRDF.md).** RULING: `SchlickBRDF::value` is the truth side, because the BRDF the `kray` convention implied, `f_impl = kray p_S/cos = S t Z p_phi/(2 nl (h.v))`, is NOT RECIPROCAL -- swapping the directions leaves `h`, `t`, `Z`, `p_phi`, `S` and `(h.v)` untouched and turns `nl` into `nv`, so `f_impl(l->v)/f_impl(v->l) = nl/nv` (measured 0.684040 / 0.394931 / 0.176327 / 0.707107 / 0.259808 at five (theta_v,theta_l) pairs, matching the prediction to 1e-9, while `value()` itself is reciprocal to 3.0e-16), and RISE runs `schlick_material` through BDPT/VCM/MLT light subpaths. **This row's own formula for the missing factor, `hl/(nv*t*A)`, is STALE**: it is derived against the pre-DL-67 azimuthal density `p_phi = A^2/(2 pi)`; Post-fix: the reverse, exactly. `GGXBRDF::value()`/`GGXSPF::Pdf()` (`return diffuse+specular;` / "3-lobe mixture PDF") confirmed the OTHER two `PTMulDiv` overwrite sites in the same block (RIS-accepted candidate, one-sample guided-direction-accepted) re-evaluate the material's AGGREGATE BSDF/PDF fresh at the priced direction. **Correction (2026-09-13, same slice, before merge): this row originally continued "...and are therefore already complete, self-contained estimators that must NOT also divide by selectProb -- applying the fix there would double-count." That claim is FALSE and is withdrawn.** `GuidingSupportsSurfaceSampling` admits only non-delta eRayDiffuse/eRayReflection lobes, so at a multi-lobe surface with an ineligible lobe (e.g. this row's own `TranslucentMaterial` EXIT fixture: diffuse-exit eligible, translucent-backscatter not) the aggregate-BSDF branches price ALL lobes while guiding only ever proposed/weighted the eligible subset, and the ineligible lobe is priced AGAIN through the ordinary kray/selectProb path -- and even restricted to an all-eligible material, per-lobe kray/pdf (this fix) and aggregate BSDF/pdf (the other two branches) are two different, mutually-inconsistent RIS/one-sample proposal measures unless the SPF has only one lobe. This is a real, still-open, DIFFERENT bug from the one this row fixes; it is filed as DL-67 (renumbered from this slice's original DL-65 at merge time to avoid colliding with concurrently-filed rows from sibling debt-cleanup slices), not fixed here. Confirmed `ggx_material`/`coated_material` cannot exercise EITHER bug (this row's or DL-67's) at all (`GGXSPF`/`CoatedSPF` each do internal single-lobe selection, emitting exactly one `ScatteredRay` per call, so the outer `selectProb` is trivially 1 for either). `BDPTIntegrator.cpp`'s analogous "kept BSDF direction" one-sample fallback (eye ~2662, light ~6363-ish) does not share DL-42's pattern (a dropped `selectProb` division): it unconditionally folds `selectProb` into `scatterPdf = selectProb*effectivePdf`. It DOES, however, always use the aggregate `PathValueOps::EvalBSDFAtVertex` paired with a PER-LOBE `effectivePdf`, never `pScat->kray` directly, for its non-delta throughput -- a THIRD, independent measure-mismatch pattern (aggregate f over per-lobe pdf, an over-count on overlapping-support multi-lobe materials rather than an under-count), filed as DL-69, not fixed here. **HWSS correction: PT's HWSS body (`IntegrateFromHitHWSS`) contains no guiding block at all (verified: zero occurrences of `pGuidingField`/`GuidingCombinedPdf`/`GuidingRIS` in that function) -- it is unaffected by this fix, not "covered via the shared RGB/NM template" as an earlier version of this row's detailed section claimed; HWSS reaches the fixed line only indirectly, via its per-wavelength fallback calls into `IntegrateFromHitNM` for BSDF materials.** | M | physics-bias | user-visible (path guiding and mixed-lobe materials) |

| ~~DL-39~~ | ~~DL01_TRANSLUCENT_EXIT_WEIGHT.md: independent residuals~~ | ~~Dedicated translucent photon deposition counts absorbed power as deposited power~~ | CLOSED 2026-09-13 — `1fe5c760`, `TranslucentPhotonEnergyTest` 8 checks / 0 failures (was 6 failures) | `1fe5c760`: `TracePhoton` now sums the diffuse lobe's own kray directly and deposits `power*diffuse_kray` instead of `power*(1-accum_scattered)`. Red-proof against the unfixed tracer: deposited power read the full unattenuated `(1,1,1)` at extinction 0.1 and 1 alike. Post-fix: deposited power equals `incoming*exp(-extinction*distance)*(1-scattering)` exactly, and `absorbed_frac+deposited_frac+traced_frac==1`. See [DL01_TRANSLUCENT_EXIT_WEIGHT.md](DL01_TRANSLUCENT_EXIT_WEIGHT.md). **Scope correction (review round 3, 2026-09-13, `fb2f75af`):** a round-2 revision had generalised the "sum the diffuse lobe's own kray" rule to EVERY diffuse deposit and asserted that as "a genuine accuracy improvement". That was wrong. `TranslucentPelPhotonMap::RadianceEstimate` is the Jensen estimator (`sum(power_i) * brdf.value(...) / (pi*r^2)`), so the STORED quantity must be the flux ARRIVING at the surface — the gather applies that surface's own BSDF itself. Depositing the diffuse kray is correct ONLY where that kray is a transport attenuation, i.e. at a translucent interior exit (`Beer * (1-scattering)`); a Lambertian wall's `eRayDiffuse` kray IS its albedo (`LambertianSPF.cpp`) and TranslucentSPF's own ENTERING-branch diffuse kray is `pRefFront`, a reflectance — storing either makes the gather read `power * albedo^2`. `TracePhoton` now detects the translucent exit via `GetSpecularInfo().hasInterior && ior_stack.containsCurrent()` (captured BEFORE the trace loop, since a recursive call that inherits the same stack re-points its current object) and keeps the pre-DL-39 `power*(1-accum_scattered)` formula at every other hit. `TranslucentPhotonEnergyTest` sub-test 3 now asserts the FULL arriving power at the Lambertian wall; red-proof against the round-2 tracer: `got (0.7,0.5,0.3), expected (1,1,1)`. Test prints `TranslucentPhotonEnergyTest: 14 checks, 0 failures`. | M | physics-bias | user-visible (translucent photon maps) |

P2-5 (review round 2): the volume sites additionally recorded the moment against the RAW `phasePdf` while the direction came from the guided mixture; `bsdfPdf` is now `effectivePdf` (the true density) and the raw phase pdf moved to the new `bsdfMisPdf` field, where DL-73 requires it -- see the DL-74 row. Gate: `RayCasterEnvEscapeMISTest` 91/91, `SSSRadianceScalingTest` 574017/0, `MISWeightsTest` 59/0, `RasterizerDefaultsConsistencyTest` 164/0, all unchanged; `make -C build/make/rise -j8 all` clean. Review round 3 (same slice): the red-proof asserted only `Solve()`'s alpha, which is a RATIO and so is unmoved by a `bsdfTimesCos` scaled by any constant -- precisely this row's failure shape. `OptimalMISAccumulator` gained a const `GetTileTraining()` accessor and `OptimalMISTrainingSitesTest` now asserts the trained QUANTITY: at the volume site every escape contributes exactly `(L_env*phasePdf)^2/phasePdf^2 = L_env^2`, so the accumulated sum must be a whole multiple of `L_env^2` not exceeding the attempt count (measured **484 x L_env^2 over 726 attempts**), and at BOTH sites re-running identical seeds under a 3x brighter uniform environment must give exactly **9x** (measured 9 and 9). Red-proved by mutating both volume sites to `rs2.bsdfTimesCos = RISEPel(1,1,1)`: sum inflates to **76430.2 = 484 x (4*PI)^2**, two assertions fail (17/2), while the alpha assertion stays green. 19/0 after. **Review round 4 (same slice) corrected the trained QUANTITY itself.** `Sw*cos` is the BSSRDF exit's integrand with the AREA-measure `weightSpatial = Rd*Ft(exit)/pdfSurface` DROPPED, and `LightSampler`'s entry-NEE arm dropped the identical factor from the other side of the same pair (the caller applies it after the call returns); the accumulator's moment is of the INTEGRAND (Kondapaneni 2019), so both halves were scaled by an arbitrary, non-O(1) amount and skewed the alpha of every tile an SSS surface touches. Since `weight = weightSpatial*Sw*PI` and `cosinePdf = cos/PI`, the integrand is exactly `weight*cosinePdf` -- a multiply, not a ratio, so `PTBssrdfSwTimesCos` becomes `PTBssrdfTrainedBsdfTimesCos(weight, cosinePdf)`, its Rd==0 guard disappears and the Pel form stays per-channel instead of collapsing to `MaxValue()`. `EvaluateDirectLighting{,NM}` gain a trailing `neeTrainingScale` (default 1) that scales the TRAINING integrand only, never the returned radiance; the two BSSRDF/random-walk entry-NEE call sites pass `PTSurvivalMagnitude(bssrdfWeightSpatial)`. The alternative offered in review -- leaving the site untrained, as DL-84 leaves PT's in-loop volume site -- was rejected because it silences the BSDF half while the NEE half, governed by a per-scene accumulator pointer rather than a per-call flag, goes on training the same mis-scaled moment UNPAIRED. Red-proof (the `L_env^2` law cannot see this -- a radiance scale multiplies right and wrong numerators alike): a decorator scales the diffusion profile's `Rd` by 2 and forwards every sampling method verbatim, the walk gets a large path importance so `RayCaster`'s own `rs.importance < RC_RR_THRESHOLD` test cannot fire for either run, and the accumulator is wired into the `LightSampler` so the NEE half is measured too; identical attempt counts confirm identical paths, and every moment must then scale by exactly 4 (bounded below 4 by the tile's Rd-independent SSS-surface events). On `145c9259` BOTH ratios were EXACTLY **1**; after, **3.99962** (BSDF) and **3.99567** (NEE). `OptimalMISTrainingSitesTest` **23/0** (21/2 before).
